#### 2024-04-01 — City Run/Walk is this Sunday, April 7! Benefits Friends of Cambridge Athletics (FOCA)

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements · <https://crlsrowing.org/city-run-walk-is-this-sunday-april-7-benefits-friends-of-cambridge-athletics-foca/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/php1Yav9j_36CityRunAH2024OUT-1-scaled.jpg]

FOCA City Run/Walk is this Sunday April 7! 

For more information, go here: https://racewire.com/register.php?id=13576 [https://racewire.com/register.php?id=13576]

5k Loop Around Scenic Fresh Pond 

This 5k loop around Fresh Pond, starts and finishes on the service road behind the water building and takes place entirely within Fresh Pond Reservation, along historic trails and pathways and verdant woods. From the water building, run clockwise towards the open gate, that takes you along the paved pathway and continue past the Golf Course and Little Fresh Pond. Take LEFT at Sousa’s Rock, heading up towards Maher Field. Take your first RIGHT, running past the soccer field and through a wooded area that returns you to the Pond. Take a LE FT on the paved pathway, continuing until you reach the front side of the water building, bearing to the right onto the wood chipped pathway that runs along the perimeter of beautiful Fresh Pond. At the top of the hill take a LEFT to finish behind the Water Building, where you started. The run is all in nature – no cars!

Registration Fees: 

Race Fee: $35 (Day-of: $45)

College Students: $25 (Day-of: $35)

CRLS/CPSD & EBHS Students & Under-18: Free

Walkers $35

CityRun Senior (65+): $30 (Day-of: $40)

CityRun Virtual: $35

Charitable Fundraiser Race: 

Fees benefit Friends of Cambridge Athletics (FOCA) , a 501C3 supporting student athletes at CRLS and awarding scholarships to deserving scholar athletes and the Andrea Harvey Memorial Fund, a non-profit advancing education and raising domestic violence awareness and offering scholarships to CRLS students and EBHS students.

Awards and T-Shirts 

T-Shirts to the first 300 registrants.

Award to top male/female finishers in the following categories:

1st Place Medals only to the following age groups 10 & under, 11-14, 15-18, 19-29, 30-39, 40-49, 50-59, 60-69, 70-79, 80 and over

Wheelchair winner

Top female/male overall, no double dipping

Bib Pick-up , T-Shirt pick up 

Pick up at Marathon Sports, 1654 Mass Ave, Cambridge:

Friday 4/5, 4:00-6:30pm

Saturday, 4/6, 11:00am-2:00 pm

Marathon offering a 20% discount on merchandise

Race-day pick up at Fresh Pond starting at 7:30am (T-shirts only guaranteed to first 300 pre-registered participants)

Pre-race information 

Please arrive by 8 am. The 5k race is entirely WITHIN Fresh Pond Reservation (no cars) and mainly on paved pathways, although a small portion of the run is on woodchipped pathways and through verdant woods, on a dirt trail.

FAQ 

Are strollers allowed?

Yes, strollers are allowed, but are asked to start behind all the runners.

Are dogs allowed?

Yes, leashed dogs are allowed, but are asked to start behind all the runners.

Where do I park?

Closest spots to PARK can be found along Huron Avenue, Lakeview, or Lexington Aves. Please use the Crosswalk @ Huron Ave and to the right of Vassal Lane/Fresh Pond Parkway. It’s a very short walk to the Water Building. Please note that TOBIN SCHOOL PARKING is NO LONGER AVAILABLE!

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/pexels-photo-2526878.jpg]

The Mayor’s Cup vs BB&N will be SATURDAY April 13!

The race will be held on the Powerhouse Course on the Charles River. The schedule is as follows:

1:30 – BJV/Novice 8

2:10 – B3V

The Powerhouse Stretch

(Thanks to Brookline Rowing for this excellent description).

Some of our races run on the “Powerhouse” stretch of the Charles: A 1000-meter straightaway starting just downstream of the Weeks footbridge and ending just below the Riverside dock and boathouse. There is excellent race viewing, and you can pick your spot to see early, middle, or late stages of the races: along the Cambridge riverbank or from either of the Western Avenue and River Street bridges.

There is no designated parking for the course, and public parking can be scarce.

#### 2024-04-26 — CRLS vs Brooks, Northfield Mount Hermon and Middlesex

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Race Schedule, Regatta Food · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls-vs-brooks-northfield-mount-hermon-and-middlesex/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/6-4.36.41 PM-4.36.41 PM.png] 

CRLS vs Brooks/NMH/Middlesex 

The boys and girls varsity boats are racing Brooks, Northfield Mount Hermon and Middlesex (girls only) tomorrow, Saturday, April 27, 2024 at Brooks [https://www.google.com/maps/place/Brooks+School/@42.707446,-71.085589,15z/data=!4m6!3m5!1s0x89e3849b6d38c49f:0xd9969340f9d6d6b1!8m2!3d42.707446!4d-71.085589!16zL20vMDVxZjIz?entry=ttu] . A campus map and draft schedule are linked below.

Please note: 

- 1V – 5V boats will be racing. Check with your athlete for the lineups.

- First race starts at 3:00pm and the last race is scheduled to start at 4:30. Draft schedule attached. A livestream of the racing can be found here: https://www.brooksschool.org/athletics/livestream [https://www.brooksschool.org/athletics/livestream]

- Spectators should enter the campus at the MAIN entrance (not the athletic entrance), and follow instructions from the parking/security attendants. There is NO PARKING at the boathouse. Campus address is 1160 Great Pond Rd, North Andover, MA [https://www.google.com/maps/place/Brooks+School/@42.707446,-71.085589,15z/data=!4m6!3m5!1s0x89e3849b6d38c49f:0xd9969340f9d6d6b1!8m2!3d42.707446!4d-71.085589!16zL20vMDVxZjIz?entry=ttu]. See the attached map for more information.

- CRITICAL: All competing athletes must arrive at CBC tomorrow between 10:45-11AM to load the trailer before boarding the buses. If they are not at CBC at 11AM they will not race because they will not have a boat!

- Buses depart from CBC at 12:00pm . As a reminder: all athletes are expected to ride the bus to and from races!

- We will be providing deli platters so that the kids can make their own bag lunches before getting on the buses. There will be vegetarian and Passover-friendly options, as well as a variety of nutritionist-recommended snacks. If these options don’t work for your athlete, please encourage them to bring food with them. Also, if you have food recommendations for our upcoming races, please feel free to share! We encourage families to volunteer with our race operations committee.

- All athletes should bring a filled water bottle and their shiny new unisuit!

See below for all race-related documents:

#### 2024-04-30 — CRLS vs Winsor and Newton Country Day, Wednesday May 1 (GIRLS ONLY)

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Race Schedule · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls-vs-winsor-and-ncd/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/CRLS-Crew-Event-IG.png] 

CRLS vs Winsor and Newton Country Day (Girls Only) 

The girls 1V – 5V will be racing Winsor and Newton Country Day School tomorrow, Wednesday, May 1, 2024.

Racing will take place in the CRI pond (i.e. the section of the river near Community Rowing on Nonantum Road, see map below ). If you’re interested in watching, the first race should start at 4:15pm. Check with your athlete for the lineups and exact race schedule. The race is about 1400m long, and starts at roughly the public boat dock past the last of the CRI docks. The boats will head upstream, past the Newton Yacht Club, and finish at the drainage pipe.

The current schedule (updated 5/1) for this afternoon’s racing is as follows:

5V – 4:15 PM

4V – 4:25 PM

3V – 4:35 PM

2V – 4:45 PM

1V – 4:55 PM

There aren’t a lot of great vantage points along the course, but if you want to see the finish, this [https://goo.gl/maps/LupBB1Ya5HsATHvi9] is a good place to stand.

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/CRLS-Crew-Event-IG-1-1.png]

CRLS vs. Middlesex [https://www.google.com/maps/place/261+Nashua+Rd,+North+Billerica,+MA+01862/@42.5303535,-71.2993292,15z/data=!4m16!1m9!3m8!1s0x89e398bac9dee571:0xaab3c881e1f5a852!2s261+Nashua+Rd,+North+Billerica,+MA+01862!3b1!8m2!3d42.5354699!4d-71.3007926!10e5!16s%2Fg%2F11cnd78wq3!3m5!1s0x89e398bac9dee571:0xaab3c881e1f5a852!8m2!3d42.5354699!4d-71.3007926!16s%2Fg%2F11cnd78wq3?entry=ttu] , Saturday 5/11

UPDATE: Following is the schedule for the races against Middlesex:

G4 – 3:00pm

B4 – 3:10pm

G3 – 3:20pm

B3 – 3:30pm

G2 – 3:40pm

B2 – 3:50pm

G1 – 4:00pm

B1 – 4:10pm 

- The girls and boys varsity boats (1V – 4V) are racing Middlesex on Saturday, May 11, 2024. First race starts at 3:00pm.

- Rowers should be at CBC at 12:00pm for trailer loading. Buses will depart for Middlesex at 1:30pm. As a reminder, all athletes are expected to ride the bus to and from races.

- The first race is currently expected to start at 3:00pm. Rowers should check with the coaches for final line-ups and schedules.

- Lunch/snacks will be available. There will be deli sandwiches (turkey and vegetarian options), along with nutritionist-approved snacks available before the kids get on the bus. If those options are not acceptable, please encourage your athlete to bring whatever food they prefer.

- All athletes should bring a filled water bottle!

- Parking [https://www.google.com/maps/place/261+Nashua+Rd,+North+Billerica,+MA+01862/@42.5303535,-71.2993292,15z/data=!4m16!1m9!3m8!1s0x89e398bac9dee571:0xaab3c881e1f5a852!2s261+Nashua+Rd,+North+Billerica,+MA+01862!3b1!8m2!3d42.5354699!4d-71.3007926!10e5!16s%2Fg%2F11cnd78wq3!3m5!1s0x89e398bac9dee571:0xaab3c881e1f5a852!8m2!3d42.5354699!4d-71.3007926!16s%2Fg%2F11cnd78wq3?entry=ttu] can be found here. Please see important information below, or at this pdf for important details on visiting Middlesex’s boathouse and race etiquette: Directions to Middlesex School Paumgarten Boathouse [https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/Directions-to-Middlesex-School-Paumgarten-Boathouse.pdf]

- Middlesex has made the following request:

Given that it is Alumni weekend for us, it may be a bit crowded in the parking lot but it is very important that cars do not park along Riveredge Rd. and that spectators do not come down the street toward the boathouse while boats are launching/racing.

Directions to Middlesex School Paumgarten Boathouse: Concord River 

- Parking

- There is no parking at the Boathouse. Parking will be available on the corner at 261 Nashua Rd [https://www.google.com/maps/place/261+Nashua+Rd,+North+Billerica,+MA+01862/@42.5303535,-71.2993292,15z/data=!4m16!1m9!3m8!1s0x89e398bac9dee571:0xaab3c881e1f5a852!2s261+Nashua+Rd,+North+Billerica,+MA+01862!3b1!8m2!3d42.5354699!4d-71.3007926!10e5!16s%2Fg%2F11cnd78wq3!3m5!1s0x89e398bac9dee571:0xaab3c881e1f5a852!8m2!3d42.5354699!4d-71.3007926!16s%2Fg%2F11cnd78wq3?entry=ttu]. There are bathrooms available for spectators at this address.

- On race days, a Police Officer is present to manage parking.

- Please do not park at the Riverview restaurant or the Marina.

- Please do not drive or park on Riveredge Rd or any adjacent neighborhood streets.

- Crews should back down, unload hulls and return vehicle to the corner lot.

- Buses need to drop off and return at conclusion of racing, they may park at Middlesex Campus.

- Race Viewing

- The course runs from midway between Rt 225 and Rt 4 bridges

- The finish line is below the Rt 4 bridge

- The best place to view the race is on the Rt 4 bridge

- Conduct

- NO DOGS are allowed. NO EXCEPTIONS.

- Our boathouse site is located in a residential neighborhood. Please be sensitive to the residents.

- Please do not drive or park on Riveredge Rd or any adjacent neighborhood streets.

Driving Directions: 

GPS address: Parking and spectators: 261 Nashua Rd, Billerica MA

Boathouse: 20 Riveredge Rd

From Middlesex School 

Exit campus and TURN right heading to Carlisle Center

Go to the rotary and go straight heading toward Bedford, MA

Take FIRST left after the rotary onto EAST Street

Bear right on East Street and follow to the set of lights at Route 4

Turn RIGHT at the lights (Route 4)

Go. 0.8 miles and park at the lot on the corner of Route 4 and Riveredge Road

From Rt 3 North or South: 

Exit at Treble Cove RD

Turn Right Off ramps

Travel to Lights at Rt 4, Nashua Rd, fire house on Right

TURN LEFT and travel 0.8 miles to lot at River Edge RD

_______________________________

The 1V-4V boats are racing at the New England Interscholastic Rowing Association (NEIRA) Championships [https://www.regattacentral.com/regatta/?job_id=9046] on Saturday, May 25, 2024 at Lake Quinsigamond, Worcester [https://www.google.com/maps/place/Regatta+Point+Park/@42.2775471,-71.758276,15.2z/data=!4m6!3m5!1s0x89e40880eed2b87b:0xc4fb70f18c0f66af!8m2!3d42.2776044!4d-71.7581704!16s%2Fg%2F1tl_05z6?entry=ttu].

These are all-day regattas, so we will be bringing the food tents and the grills. We are planning on serving breakfast, lunch and snacks, menu TBD

Livestream (pay $10 to subscribe then cancel after the regatta)

https://www.overnght.com/ [https://www.overnght.com/]

Live Results can be found here:

https://neira.qra.org/ [https://neira.qra.org/]

Neira heat sheets can be found here: 2024 NEIRA Heats [https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/2024-NEIRA-Heats.pdf]. The first heat is scheduled for 8:00am, and the last final is 4:42pm 

This is the first time that ALL FOUR (1V-4V) BOATS from both the girls’ and boys’ teams have qualified for NEIRA. As you may know, NEIRA schools are seeded based on their performance over the spring season, and have to qualify for the championships. Given the breadth and resources of our competition, which are primarily prep schools, this is a huge deal for our squad, and we should be extremely proud of our kids and their coaches.

#### 2024-05-25 — CRLS at MPSRA, Sunday May 26

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Race Schedule · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls-at-mpsra/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/CRLS-Crew-MPSRA-2024.png]

CRLS at MPSRA, Sunday May 26

UPDATE (see live results and livestream below):

The entire squad (novice and varsity) is racing at the Massachusetts Public School Rowing Association (MPSRA or Mass Pubs) Spring Championship [https://www.regattacentral.com/regatta/?job_id=8602] on Sunday, May 26, 2024 in Lowell [https://www.google.com/maps/place/UMass+Lowell+Bellegarde+Boathouse/@42.6405384,-71.355006,17z/data=!3m2!4b1!5s0x89e3a490cac25765:0xe83ea94cfa750a5f!4m6!3m5!1s0x89e3a490ca414f71:0x8a186d015a72ccb6!8m2!3d42.6405384!4d-71.3524311!16s%2Fg%2F11df1v089f?entry=ttu]. 

Watch our novice and JV crews help our varsity bring the MPSRA State Championship trophies back home on Sunday. The results for that race are live https://legacy.herenow.com/results/#/races/21232/schedule [https://legacy.herenow.com/results/#/races/21232/schedule] but there is no livestream so come in person to watch! These are all-day regattas, so we will be bringing the food tents and the grills. We are planning on serving breakfast, lunch and snacks, menu TBD.

Heat sheets for Mass Pubs are not available yet, but based on last year’s schedule, we expect the first heat to start at 8:00am and the last final to be around 3:30pm. 

We are asking for a lot of help this weekend, but I want to emphasize how important these races are. Mass Pubs are our state championship, and the squad has done exceptionally well here in the past. This is the first major race in which the entire squad competes, and they need our support.

#### 2024-06-01 — CRLS Historic Weekend Recap – STATE CHAMPIONS

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Results · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls-historic-weekend-recap-state-champions/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/06/DSC_8402_Original.heic] [https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/06/DSC_8402_Original.heic] [https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/06/DSC_8402_Original-1.heic] [https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/06/IMG_7356-scaled.jpeg]

Over the course of two separate Championship regattas, and 33 (!) races in 31.5 hours, it was truly a Historic weekend for CRLS Crew.

Saturday: New England Interscholastic Rowing Association Championships

This League consists of 42 New England public and (primarily) private school teams. Teams must qualify their crew in their division based on race results throughout the season. CRLS was one of two public schools to qualify for this year’s Championship.

For the first time in CRLS History:

ALL 4 Boys Crews Qualified for the Championship Regatta

ALL 8 CRLS Crews Qualified for the Championship Regatta

2 Crews advanced to the Grand Finals (Boys 4V and Boys 3V)

5 Total Crews advanced to a Final (Boys 4V, 3V, 2V, and Girls 2V and 1V)

First CRLS Crew to Finish 1st in a Final (Girls 1V, Petite Final)

The Boys 4V and 3V are ranked 6th in New England among their respective categories

The Girls and Boys 2V are ranked 10th in New England in their respective categories

The Girls 1V is ranked 7th in New England

Sunday: Massachusetts Public School Rowing Association State Championships vs Arlington Belmont, Boston Latin, Bromfield Acton Boxborough, Brookline, Haverhill, Hingham, Medford, Row Boston, Somerville, Shrewsbury, Lowell, Wayland Weston 

71 CRLS Student-Athletes

14 CRLS Crews

20 Races

30 Gold Medals

30 Silver Medals

1 First Place Petite Final Finish

2 First Place Cups

2 Team Points Trophies

The Varsity and Novice Girls medaled in all of their 4+ events, earning the Girls Fours Team Points Trophy. Special shout out to the Girls 2nd Novice 4+ for winning Gold in their event, and to the Girls Novice 8+ for crossing the line 1st in their Petite final.

The Boys swept all of their events, earning the Boys Fours Team Points Trophy, the Novice 4+ taking home the 1st Novice 4+ Cup, and the Varsity 4+ taking the Title of STATE CHAMPIONS!!! 

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/06/DSC_8402_Original-scaled.jpeg]

#### 2024-08-02 — Registration for CRLS Crew, Fall 2024: Deadline is August 13!

*Author: Mary Kay Kenney · Categories: Announcements, Season Registration · Tags: registration · <https://crlsrowing.org/fall-24-registration/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/CRLS-Crew-Slider-6-x-6-in-1.png]

Register NOW for Fall Crew 2024: Deadline is August 13!

Please note: the deadline is firm! To participate, you must have completed the following items.

VARSITY AND NOVICE: 

- Register to be on the team here: https://secure1.cpsd.us/athletics/ [https://secure1.cpsd.us/athletics/]

- Have an up-to-date physical exam and upload it to the registration portal.

- Complete an online Concussion course and Implicit Bias course here: https://nfhslearn.com/courses [https://nfhslearn.com/courses] and upload the certificates to the portal.

- After registration, new athletes should have received information about baseline ImPact tests via your SCHOOL email. Complete the test and email the ImPact certificate and please include your sport in the email subject. Impact test certificate is the only piece of paperwork that should be emailed to the school trainers. This baseline is required every 2 years. When these first 4 items are complete, please send a note to the trainers that everything is complete and uploaded.

- New athletes must sign a waiver with the Cambridge Boat Club [https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/CRLS-Waiver-and-Release-rev-07-21-22.pdf]. Following completion, please email to crls@cambridge-boat-club.org [mailto:crls@cambridge-boat-club.org]

- All athletes are required to join USRowing https://membership.usrowing.org/ [https://membership.usrowing.org/] at least at a Basic Membership level and sign their online waiver in order to compete with the team (Team code is BSK4V). This can be done now (cost is $25 for Basic) or you can wait until after the first week of school when we register new athletes as a group through the athletic department. Parents and guardians will still need to sign the online waiver after their athlete is signed up. 

NOVICE ONLY: 

- A swim test is scheduled during pre-season for novices. The test is to swim 2 lengths of the pool and 10 minutes of treading water/staying afloat. If a student cannot pass, we will have personal flotation devices on hand for them to wear while they are in the boat. We encourage students to work on swimming proficiency and the lifeguards are happy to re-test later in the season.

#### 2024-08-08 — Boys Fall Crew 2024: Important Updates

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Boys Varsity, Preseason, Season Registration, Tryouts · Tags: boys varsity, preseason, tryouts · <https://crlsrowing.org/boys-fall-crew-2024-important-updates/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/CRLS-Crew-Facebook-Post-2.png]

Hi Everyone,

This post contains important information regarding Fall Varsity Boys Crew tryouts. Please read carefully.

Due to a high number of registrations, there will be a significant number of cuts through tryouts than in the past. This means many of you will not be able to train on the water with the team this season even if you have participated in one or or even six prior seasons.

Here are the factors and criteria the boys varsity coaches will take into consideration during the tryouts process:

1. All registration paperwork has been successfully submitted to the school by 8/13

2. Demonstrated commitment to the team

a. Will attend all practices and all races with limited exceptions

b. Previous seasons on varsity

3. Physical fitness: Thursday, September 5th, 3:30 PM at Russell Field, athletes will complete a 30 minute erg piece. The scores will be weight adjusted. The top 20 athletes will be given extra consideration during the tryout process.

4. Technical Skills: Willingness to learn and ability to follow instructions

5. Behavior, Attitude, Good Sportsmanship

TRYOUT DATES: Tryouts will start on Tuesday, September 3rd at CBC and will end on Friday, September 6th. 

PRESEASON: PRESEASON IS NOT TRYOUTS. Think of preseason as a free two week sweeps rowing camp to help you get ready for showing up 100% for tryouts week

We look forward to seeing you all soon.

-CRLS Varsity Boys Crew Coaching Staff

This Saturday, September 7, 2024, we will be holding the fall season family orientation session at the Cambridge Boat Club (CBC) at 12:00pm. We will introduce the coaches and the parent board members, discuss expectations for student-athletes for the season, go over opportunities for families to support the team, and have a tour of CBC for families new to the team. #### 2024-09-20 — CRLS at the CRI Fall Classic, Saturday, September 21

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Race Schedule · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls_fall_classic/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/CRLS-Crew-Event-IG.png]

CRLS at the CRI Fall Classic 

The fall racing season kicks off tomorrow, with the varsity squads competing at the CRI Fall Classic [https://communityrowing.org/event/fall-classic-regatta/]. For those of you who are new to the sport, CRI is Community Rowing, an amazing program located up the river in Brighton. We can’t say enough about the work that CRI has done to transform the sport, so we urge you all to learn more about it here [https://communityrowing.org/]. Also, if you’re interested in learning how to row, CRI is the place to go!

All athletes should check in with their coaches for any last minute instructions, but the schedule is as follows: girls and the boys’ 2x should arrive at CBC no later than 9:00am . The rest of the boys need to be at CBC at 11:00am.

We will have some fruit for the kids, but it is the responsibility of each athlete to fuel up appropriately before arrival.(For families with questions about nutrition and rowing, please visit this link [https://crlsrowing.org/teamonly/].)

We have 5 girls 4+s, 5 boys 4+s, and 1 boys 2x racing. Race schedule as follows:

- 10:09am – Men’s Jr. 2x 

- 10:49am – Women’s Jr. 4+ 

- 12:40pm – Men’s Jr. 4+ 

Heat sheets are available here [https://legacy.herenow.com/results/#/races/21276/heats]. Check with your athlete for lineup details.

The race starts between Weeks and Anderson (the section of the river between Harvard and HBS) and runs upstream, finishing at CRI. A map of the course, including the best viewing sites, is attached and available here [chrome-extension://efaidnbmnnnibpcajpcglclefindmkaj/https://communityrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Fall-Classic-Map.pdf].

Results will be available here [https://legacy.herenow.com/results/#/races/21276/results].

#### 2024-09-27 — (untitled)

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Race Schedule · <https://crlsrowing.org/8021-2/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/CRLS-Crew-Event-IG-1-1.png]

Hello CRLS Crew families!

As a reminder, the varsity boats only will be scrimmaging tomorrow against Newton Country Day, Winsor, Narragansett, and the Riverside Juniors. (Boston Latin dropped out, so the scrimmage got a new name: The Head Lamp!)

What you need to know:

- The teams will be racing in head race style, so all boats will start single file and race against the clock.

- The race course is the Head of the Charles course, which starts at the BU Boathouse and ends upstream from CBC across from Herter Park

- The boats will cross the start line in 20 second centers (increments). First boat starts at 9:55am. Last boat starts at 10:05. The order is included below.

- Depending on the size and speed of the crew, it should take between 18-25 minutes for each boat to cross the finish line.

- Best places to watch are on the bridges: River, Western, Weeks, Anderson and Eliot. There are also numerous benches all along the bike path on the river that provide excellent vantage points.

- CBC is holding a membership meeting during the race, so unfortunately we will not be able to watch from the boathouse.

- Because this is a local, informal scrimmage, we will NOT have a team tent or provide lunch, so we will not need volunteers. As with last week’s CRI Fall Classic, we will provide the kids with fruit before the race and some sugary carbs after.

Good luck to the team!

Courtney

CRLS Crew Parent Board member

#### 2024-09-30 — Textile River Regatta is this Sunday, October 6, 2024

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Regatta, Regatta Food · <https://crlsrowing.org/textile-river-regatta-is-this-sunday-october-6-2024/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/CRLS-Crew-Event-IG-2.png]

The team will be competing at the Textile River Regatta [https://www.textileriverregatta.org/2024/] this Sunday, October 6, 2024. 

The Regatta is a popular fall head race on the Merrimack River in Lowell, Massachusetts. The 2024 regatta will be held on Sunday, October 6. The TRR is a 3.5 mile race that includes a variety of sculling and sweep events for all boat classes, from juniors to masters. The event is organized by the Merrimack River Rowing Association (MRRA) in collaboration with the UMass-Lowell and Lowell High School Rowing programs. The TRR is a great place for athletes to train before the Head of the Charles Regatta. The first TRR was held in 1980, following the revival of the University of Lowell rowing program. Information on parking can be found here. [https://www.textileriverregatta.org/2024/] 

This will be the first race for the novices, so we are busting out the food tent! For those of you who are new to the team, fall races are all day events! Regatta organizers are still working on the event schedule, but the team should expect to leave CBC early in the morning (think: 5:30am) and return late afternoon. I’ll send more logistics (race schedule, parking info, results page, etc.) out later this week, but for now let’s talk about food and how we can all support the team.

For all-day races, we set up a food tent and serve the kids breakfast and lunch, and provide healthy snacks throughout the day, with slightly less healthy snacks for the bus ride home. S ome notes about food at regattas:

- We have traditionally provided the team with snacks and meals at regattas so that everyone on the team is fueled up and ready to perform at their best. Race days are long (10-12 hours) and there are rarely food vendors readily available. 

- We spend roughly $1500 – $3000 per regatta to feed everyone. The total cost per race will vary depending on the number of kids racing, the length of the day, and what’s on the menu. Total mouths to feed can be 100-130+ people. 

- Neither the school nor FOCA provide food or funds to feed the team. All race day meals/snacks are organized by the parent board, served by family volunteers, and paid for by the CRLS Crew families. We rely on the support of families to feed the team. 

- If you are able to contribute to our food fund, we would appreciate it. Please know that no family is required to pay anything, and that NO ATHLETE WILL EVER BE DENIED FOOD. If, however, you are in a position to contribute money to cover others, we will happily accept. If you would like to talk further about suggested donation amounts, or how we handle this, please feel free to reach out. 

Let’s talk about the menu! This weekend we are having bagels and yogurt with fruit and honey for breakfast, and burgers (meat and veggie) for lunch. We will have plenty of veggies and dips, as well as healthy snacks suggested by our guest nutritionist [https://crlsrowing.org/caregiversandteam/teamonly/nutrition/]. If this menu presents significant challenges for your rower due to allergies or dietary restrictions, please reach out ASAP! Also, if you’re a foodie and have some ideas that will put this menu to shame, please join our food committee! We have another big regatta at the end of the month and we will happily put you to work!

Now on to the fun part: working the tent! A Signup Genius for this weekend is available here [https://www.signupgenius.com/go/60B0F4CAAAA2CA31-51871794-textile]. Job descriptions are below. Board member Carroll will be the main point of contact for logistics this weekend, and she, along with our esteemed colleagues Melissa and Amy, will lead a Zoom meeting this Wednesday to go over volunteer duties. This is a great race to both volunteer for and watch, and there will be plenty of experienced volunteers around to answer questions.

- Shoppers. Do you like to shop? If so, this job is for you. We provide a very detailed list, and you can order online or shop in-person at BJs or Costco. You will need a membership at one or the other. After you get the food, you can drop it off with Carroll. Ideally this happens Friday or Saturday. Save your receipts and we will reimburse you promptly!

- Supply Transport/Site Set-up/Breakfast Service. 5:00am – 9:00am Are you an early morning person? Great! We need 6 people with big cars to pick up our site supplies from our trailer on New Street on SATURDAY, and then drive up to Lowell EARLY SUNDAY MORNING and set up our tents. You will then lay out breakfast for the kids. PLEASE EXPECT TO BE IN LOWELL AROUND 5:30AM. Breakfast will be prepared while the kids are unloading the boat trailer and rigging boats, and served around 6:30am (please note that these times are dependent on the final race schedule, which should be available on Wednesday-Thursday).

- Lunch . 10:00am – 1:00pm If you like food but don’t like to get up at the crack of dawn, please consider working the lunch shift. Lunch workers help clear out the breakfast stuff and then focus on the grilling! These volunteers also replenish the snacks.

- Clean-up/Supply Transport 2:00pm – 5:00pm The clean-up crew strikes the tents, packs up the supplies, and loads everything back into their cars for the return to our supply trailer on New Street. We will meet you at the trailer and put everything away until next time.

- Dishwashers. This one is for the clean freaks! Take home our knives and spatulas and bowls and random cooking stuff and give them a good scrub. Bring everything back to CBC at your leisure, or reach out and we will come pick everything up!

- Contributions . This is an important job. As noted above, money to feed the team comes from our families. If you are able to support our efforts, please do. We’ve included a dollar amount on the sign-up, but will accept any amount, and if you are able to contribute enough to cover others, fantastic! We are so grateful for the generosity of the CRLS Crew families! Cash or Venmo (@Amy-Moldrem) are welcome.

#### 2024-10-02 — Textile River Regatta: Sunday, October 6, 2024 – IMPORTANT UPDATES

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Race Schedule, Regatta · <https://crlsrowing.org/textile-river-regatta-sunday-october-6-2024-important-updates/>*

Here is the information you need for this weekend:

Textile River Regatta – Sunday, October 6, 2024 – PLEASE READ ALL INFORMATION CAREFULLY! 

This weekend the varsity and novice squads are rowing at the Textile River Regatta. [https://www.textileriverregatta.org/2024/] This is a 4,500 meter head race located on the Merrimack River in Lowell, MA.

The schedule for the day is included below. Lineups will be provided by the coaches directly to the athletes, so please check in with your rower to see which boats they are in. Please note that the event schedule may change in the event of weather. Bookmark the event page [https://www.textileriverregatta.org/2024/] for the latest information throughout the weekend.

BUSES: 

All rowers are expected to ride the bus to and from the regatta . Bus departure from and estimated arrival at CBC is as follows: 

5:45 AM – Athletes arrive at CBC

6:00 AM – Bus departs from CBC

Departure times are ESTIMATED!

4:30 PM – Bus Departs Race Course

5:15 PM – Arrive at CBC, all rowers unload trailer

6:15 PM – Dismissed

Please note that all rowers are expected to remain at CBC until the trailer is fully unloaded and all equipment is put away. 

Per CRLS policy, all athletes are expected to ride the bus to/from races. Any family that needs to drive their own child must submit an Athletic Transportation Release Form to the CRLS Athletics Department at least 48 hours in advance (so, before 6:00am on Friday, 10/4) The form is attached. Please send it to the coach and the athletic director, not me!

EVENT SCHEDULE – as of PM 10/2: 

9:15AM Men’s Junior 4+ “B” – Event 17, Four entries (2V – 5V)

9:25AM Women’s Junior 4+ “B” – Event 18, Four entries (2V – 5V)

11:25AM Men’s Junior 4+ “A” – Event 33

11:55AM Men’s Junior Novice 8+ – Event 36

12:40PM Women’s Junior 4+ “A” – Event 39

2:20 PM Women’s Junior Novice 8+ – Event 58

Please note that regattas can sometimes get off schedule for various reasons (weather, personnel changes, emergencies). Please visit https://www.textileriverregatta.org/2024/ [https://www.textileriverregatta.org/2024/] for the latest regatta information. 

WHAT TO BRING: 

Athletes need: Unisuit, Hat, Sunglasses, Sunscreen, Filled Water Bottle, Dry Socks, Running shoes, Homework. Hammocks are optional but handy! 

Spectators need: Reusable coffee/hot chocolate cup, reusable water bottle, folding camp chair (optional but recommended!)

FOOD: 

We are serving fruit, bagels, granola and yogurt for breakfast, and burgers and veggie burgers for lunch . There will be snacks for the kids throughout the day. If this doesn’t work for your athlete, please feel free to pack them a lunch! We also welcome menu planning assistance in the future. Please bring reusable water bottles!

PARKING AND DIRECTIONS: 

Parking information can be found here [https://www.textileriverregatta.org/2024/] and here [https://lowellregattas.com/venue-directions/parking-information/]. Parking is $20, cash only, for the main lot. The address of the main (cash) parking lot is 381 Pawtucket Boulevard Lowell, MA 01854 [https://www.google.com/maps/place/381+Pawtucket+Blvd,+Lowell,+MA+01854/@42.6413245,-71.3509691,17z/data=!3m1!4b1!4m5!3m4!1s0x89e3a491a73c2a45:0x4903678595fa3fe1!8m2!3d42.6413245!4d-71.3509691]. Do not park at Dunkin or along Pawtucket Blvd. You will be towed. There is also a free lot, but expect a walk to the action.

The regatta site has some helpful information about routes to take at the end of the day, when all of the trailers, athletes and spectators are leaving at the same time.

CARPOOLING:

Families interested in carpooling to the boathouse and/or the race course can coordinate by using our new family directory! The family directory is located on the password-protected Caregivers + Team section of our website. [https://crlsrowing.org/caregiversandteam/]

WHERE TO WATCH: 

We will have food and changing tents set up at the course. We have requested space in Area A on the below map. Look for our tents with the falcon on the sides, or an orange Works pickup truck and/or the grey CRLS boat trailer. The tents will be as close to the trailer as possible.

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/image.png]

RESULTS: 

Medals are awarded to the top three finishers in each event. Additionally, medals are awarded to crews who finish in the top three of the scholastic programs in the event. This may mean winning two medals if a scholastic team places in the top three overall finishers. Real-time results may be found on the HereNow [https://herenow.com/] site. (I’ll send the live link on Sunday.)

VOLUNTEERS: 

Parents/guardians who have offered via the SignUp Genius to help with CRLS Crew race operations, including set-up, clean-up, food prep and food service, will be contacted separately by board members Carroll and Melissa. Thank you all very much for your assistance! If you are still looking to help out, Textile organizers are in need of volunteers. Please sign up here [https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLSfIt5Rn0eXe_e3OlnkEtua1QeoOrM72jyZy6aNjvukk0N7GAQ/viewform]to work for the regatta. Ideally each school provides 1-2 volunteers! 

VENMO: 

We are still happily accepting contributions to the feeding of our team. If you are able to give, we take cash and Venmo (@Amy-Moldrem, 4120). As a reminder, NO ONE WILL EVER BE TURNED AWAY FROM OUR FOOD TENT! Thank you to everyone for your generosity

Questions? Feel free to reach out [http://MailTo: courtneyawilson@gmail.com]. Good luck to the team! Row hard, Falcons!

#### 2024-10-06 — CRLS Crew – That’s a Wrap on Textile

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Regatta · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls-crew-thats-a-wrap-on-textile/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/CRLS-Crew-Event-IG-4.png]

Do you hear that noise? That’s the clanging of medals! CONGRATULATIONS to all of our kids for rowing hard and supporting one another! For those who came home with shiny new medals, WELL DONE! Families, please make sure to ooh and ahh over the hardware our team brought home! Thank you, coaches, for everything that you do! Results are available here [https://legacy.herenow.com/results/#/races/21275/results].

The following crews brought home medals at this Regatta:

MENS JR 4+, B, 1st place – 2V

MENS JR 4+, B, 3rd place – 3V

WOMENS JR 4+, B, 1st place – 3V

MENS JR Novice 8+, 1st place (in their first race ever) 

The next race is Head of the Charles, and while we don’t traditionally set up any sort of team tent, there will be details forthcoming about how to watch.

Our next race with the full team is the MPSRA Fall Championship, on Sunday, 10/27. Volunteer signups will go out soon. In the meantime, thank you to everyone, and excellent racing to the kids!

#### 2024-10-14 — CRLS at Head of the Charles 2024

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Regatta · Tags: HOCR · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls-at-head-of-the-charles-2024/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/354x66_HOCR-Header2.png] 

The Head of the Charles Regatta (the largest regatta in the world!) starts this Friday (10/18) here at our home on the Charles River. Our CRLS crews (2 boys and 2 girls crews) are racing, plus the coaches for CRLS Crew will race SIX times (plus one of our coach’s partners will also race)! 

The races can be viewed through livestream hocr.org/the-regatta/livestreams [http://hocr.org/the-regatta/livestreams] (starting Friday at 7 AM), but there is nothing more fun than cheering for our CRLS Crews on the banks of the Charles River while they compete in this prestigious race in our own backyard. The greatest views are from the bridges (particularly Weeks, Anderson, and Eliot), and from CRLS Crew’s home at the Cambridge Boat Club (CBC). If you would like to watch at CBC, tell the attendants at the gate in the CBC parking lot the name of your student-athlete (even if they are not competing) and you will be let in.

*Be aware it will be hard/impossible to find parking near the river.* 

Results can be found here. [https://www.regattacentral.com/regatta/results2/?job_id=8995&org_id=0]

The 3-mile race starts below the BU Bridge at the BU Boathouse and finishes upstream of the Eliot Bridge/CBC across from Christian Herter Park/Community Garden/Amphitheater.

• 8:05 am: Joan Campbell (Coach Bill’s wife): Women’s Grand Veteran 80+ 1x (Cambridge Boat Club, bow 18) [https://www.regattacentral.com/regatta/results2/eventResults.jsp?job_id=8995&event_id=52]

#### 2024-10-18 — CRLS Crew – HOCR Update – 10/18/2024

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls-crew-hocr-update-10-18-2024/>*

Welcome to one of the biggest weekends in rowing!

Today the largest three day regatta in the world – the Head of the Charles Regatta (HOCR) [https://hocr.org/] – kicks off. We will have four varsity boats competing on Sunday, as well as a plethora of CRLS coaches and friends. All CRLS families are invited to support the team at Cambridge Boat Club; info about access and race times is included below. If you have never been to HOCR, we encourage you to come down to the banks of the Charles and experience it first hand. We promise that you will be impressed with both the athleticism and the spectacle of it all!

The details for HOCR can be found here [https://crlsrowing.org/crls-at-head-of-the-charles-2024/], and here are additional reminders for the weeks coming up.

- We are looking for one or two volunteers to pick up some fruit/snacks for the kids who are racing this weekend. Please shoot us a quick email [http://MailTo: courtneyawilson@gmail.com] if you can do this. 

- Next weekend is another big one for the entire squad! Mass Publics (our state public school championships) is Sunday, 10/27. It’s another all-day event up in Lowell, so we will need plenty of volunteers. The Signup Genius is going out tonight! 

- The Halloween Regatta is also coming! We will need some candy donations! Info will be sent out next week.

- Our pancake breakfast fundraiser will be Sunday, 11/3. This is the official end of our fall season! Hold the date and stay tuned for more info.

#### 2024-10-25 — Updates regarding Mass Publics this Sunday 10/27

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Regatta · <https://crlsrowing.org/updates-regarding-mass-publics-this-sunday-10-27/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/Screenshot-2024-10-25-at-5.37.02 PM.png] 

As a reminder, the entire squad is competing at Mass Pubs on Sunday in Lowell, MA [https://www.google.com/maps/place/UMass+Lowell+Bellegarde+Boathouse/@42.6405384,-71.355006,17z/data=!3m1!4b1!4m6!3m5!1s0x89e3a490ca414f71:0x8a186d015a72ccb6!8m2!3d42.6405384!4d-71.3524311!16s%2Fg%2F11df1v089f?entry=ttu&g_ep=EgoyMDI0MTAyMy4wIKXMDSoASAFQAw%3D%3D]. Expect to pay $20 cash to park.

All athletes should be at CBC no later than 6:45am on Sunday. Buses depart promptly at 7:00am. All team members are expected to ride the bus to and from the race, per CRLS policy.

Final lineups will be circulated tonight or tomorrow morning, but expect it to be a relatively short day, with all racing done by 12:30ish. Schedule is available here [https://legacy.herenow.com/results/#/races/21280/schedule].

For those still looking for a volunteer role, please consider helping the team by signing up here [https://www.signupgenius.com/go/60B0F4CAAAA2CA31-52592928-mpsra#/], or help out regatta organizers by signing up here [https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLSeKM5VNCJtjwg0KcegSUjBUH_Hs8Ljy43DkqmTgDCoyLQpVBw/viewform] . Specific details about job duties have been (or will be, if you’re just signing up now) circulated separately. Your race directors for Sunday are Carroll Magill and Amy Moldrem.

Dress warm, bring a reusable water bottle, and get ready to cheer!

#### 2024-10-28 — CRLS Crew – Weekly Update #1 – 10/28/2024

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Results · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls-crew-weekly-update-1-10-28-2024/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/Results-MPSRA-FALL-CRLS-Crew-2024.png]

What a way to close out our season! In case you couldn’t hear over the cheering and the clanging of medals, our team SWEPT the varsity fours events and came home from the state public school fall rowing championship (MPSRA) with FOUR TROPHIES, including the girls and boys 1V trophies and the girls and boys 4s team trophies! And let’s not forget about our novices! Our boys novice 8+s demolished the field in both the Ist and 2nd races, and our girls novice 4+s placed second in the 1st and 2nd races (the second novice 4 was so fast they would have taken bronze in the first novice race!), with the girls novice 8+ finishing a fast fourth in their race!

So, in summary, a lot of boats raced, a lot of medals were won, FOUR trophies are now proudly displayed in the CRLS trophy case, and we are in great shape for the spring season!

I’m working on the next email with info about this week, but I had to take a moment to congratulate our team. They worked hard and rowed hard, and we are all so proud! Stay tuned for the next update, and in the meantime, head over to our social media [https://www.instagram.com/crlscrew/] and check out the recap!

#### 2025-04-10 — Mayor’s Cup vs BB&N, Saturday April 12

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Regatta · <https://crlsrowing.org/mayors-cup-vs-bbn-saturday-april-12/>*

Good morning, CRLS Crew families –

Here are your updates for the week.

MAYOR’S CUP 

The varsity racing season kicks off this Saturday, April 12 with the Mayor’s Cup . Racing vs. BBN will happen in the Powerhouse Stretch. If you’re new to the team, the Powerhouse is the straight section of the river between the Weeks footbridge and Magazine Beach. The course runs downstream (towards BU) and the best places to watch are from the River St. and Western Ave. bridges, or at Magazine Beach, between the river and the DCR pool. Racing is expected to start at 10:00am and will likely be over by 12:00pm. The exact order of the events is TBD; I will push out the schedule as soon as I get confirmation from the coaches. Athletes should check in with their coaches for lineups and arrival times at the boathouse. Novices are welcome to watch but are not required to attend.

As the Mayor’s Cup is a small, local event, we will only have snacks (think fruit and donuts) available post-racing. Please make sure your rower has a good meal before they arrive at the boathouse.

UNISUITS 

Distribution of racing unis is almost complete; the novice girls are getting theirs very soon!

A few notes about the unisuits:

- Unis should be washed on gentle and line dried. NEVER NEVER NEVER put a unisuit in the dryer; they will shrink and thin out. Always hang them to dry! They are spandex and will dry quickly!

- All athletes should wear undergarments under their unis, especially if they are borrowing a team uni or plan to donate theirs at the end of the season. The CRLS Athletic Department requires athletes borrowing anything spandex to wear undergarments. 

- If your athlete purchased their own uni, we recommend that they label it so that it does not get lost. Metallic Sharpies work well.

- If your athlete has left the team but did not return their uni, you should receive a direct message from parent board member Melissa about how to return the uni. We have a limited number of unis and a large team, so thanks for getting them back to us!

SCHOOL VACATION 

Currently, teams will be practicing at their regular scheduled practice time. Any changes to those times will be communicated directly to the athletes by the coaches. There is a race on Saturday, April 26, which is the last weekend of the break. If your rower will not be in town that weekend, they should tell their coach ASAP!

SPRING FUNDRAISER 

As a reminder, we are hoping to raise $5K to kick off our spring regatta operations. Please see the attached flyer and give if you are able! You can send checks to FOCA [https://friendsofcambridgeathletics.org/], or use their Venmo (below). Please be sure to note that your donation is for CRLS Crew .

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/image.png]

END OF SEASON BBQ – SAVE THE DATE 

Our team BBQ will be held on Saturday, May 31, 2025. Details to come, but please hold 11:00am – 2:00pm-ish for our end of season celebration.

Questions? Please feel free to reach out.

Best,

Courtney

#### 2025-04-15 — Mayor’s Cup vs BB&N (rescheduled), Wednesday April 16 GIRLS ONLY

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Girls Varsity, Regatta · <https://crlsrowing.org/mayors-cup-vs-bbn-april16/>*

A couple of quick updates for the week,

First, the Mayor’s Cup races have been rescheduled to this Wednesday (tomorrow). April 16, 2025 at 4:00 – 5:00pm for the girls, and next Wednesday, April 23, 2025 at 2:45pm for the boys. The girls will race BB&N, while the boys will race BB&N and Thayer. All racing will take place in the Powerhouse, downstream starting at Weeks and ending at Magazine Beach. Check in with your athlete for lineups.

Next. for those looking for a summer rowing opportunity for their athlete, I highly recommend the following:

Summer Youth Sculling Skills Program 

CBC will again this summer offer a Learn2Scull program with a new name (see above) and a slightly different format. While the program in the past has focused on learning to scull, we are finding that some of our students are already competent scullers looking for more advanced sculling instruction. We will emphasize learning to scull in the morning session and advanced instruction in the afternoon. As usual, the program is open to all rising high/prep-schoolers in the Boston community. You can find all the details on the CBC website [https://458rl1jp.r.us-east-1.awstrack.me/L0/https:%2F%2Fcambridge-boat-club.org%2Fpage%2Fyouth-sculling/1/0100019615a4b370-0acf3d94-5ad7-48d3-8310-07be3ab8ac97-000000/Vp-YpXkhFtUHbfsTicGkrEjQgf0=421]. Coach Lane and Coach Mary Kay are the teachers.

Questions? Please feel free to reach out.

#### 2025-05-01 — CRLS Crew – Lunch Volunteers Needed for Groton, this Saturday, May 3, 2025

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Volunteers · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls-crew-lunch-volunteers-needed-for-groton-this-saturday-may-3-2025/>*

Hello, CRLS Crew families!

As the lineups and details for the race against Groton are finalized, I wanted to put out another call for help with food. From our amazing race directors:

We need 2-4 volunteers to help with food prep this weekend. 

• 1 person with a Costco membership who can buy the food (We will provide a detailed list and promptly reimburse you.) 

• 2-3 people who can assemble the sandwiches and bag them up Saturday morning before the rowers depart (takes about 1.5 hrs). We need a host, sandwich assemblers and someone to drive the sandwiches to CBC and distribute from the trunk of their car as kids board the bus. Should take 2/2.5 hrs total. 

This is a great way to support the team even if you can’t make it to the Saturday race! Making the sandwiches ourselves takes a little time, but helps keep costs low and our athletes well fueled.

If you are able to help out, please let me know [http://MailTo:%20courtneyawilson@gmail.com/] and we will set you up with detailed instructions. As a reminder, there will be regattas every weekend through the end of the month, so we need everyone to pitch in!

Thanks,

Courtney

#### 2025-05-02 — CRLS Crew – Weekly Update 5/2/2025 – GROTON INFO

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Race Schedule, Regatta, Regatta Food, Volunteers · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls-crew-weekly-update-5-2-2025-groton-info/>*

Hello, CRLS Crew families –

Tomorrow, the varsity boats will be racing Groton [https://www.google.com/maps/place/282+Farmers+Row,+Groton,+MA+01450/@42.5950187,-71.5983407,581m/data=!3m2!1e3!4b1!4m6!3m5!1s0x89e3ea9fd81f6e89:0x860ab68014062ff8!8m2!3d42.5950187!4d-71.5983407!16s%2Fg%2F11rwm0s2vy?entry=ttu&g_ep=EgoyMDI1MDQyOS4wIKXMDSoASAFQAw%3D%3D]. The race schedule is below.

- Buses leave CBC at 11:15am; all athletes should be at the boathouse by 10:45am.

- All athletes should bring a filled water bottle. Also, weather forecasts are still iffy, so please make sure your rower has extra dry clothes.

- Pre-made bag lunches with deli sandwiches (turkey and vegetarian options), along with nutritionist-approved snacks available before the kids get on the bus. If those options are not acceptable, please encourage your athlete to bring whatever food they prefer. If you know your rower will not be taking a bag lunch, please let us know ASAP so that we don’t waste food! 

- Thank you very much to the volunteers who will be handling lunch!

The official address for Groton [https://www.google.com/maps/place/282+Farmers+Row,+Groton,+MA+01450/@42.5950187,-71.5983407,17z/data=!3m1!4b1!4m6!3m5!1s0x89e3ea9fd81f6e89:0x860ab68014062ff8!8m2!3d42.5950187!4d-71.5983407!16s%2Fg%2F11rwm0s2vy?entry=ttu] is 282 Farmers Row, Groton , MA 01450. The boathouse and docks are located some distance from the parking area, so be prepared to walk. There is no parking at the boathouse! 

Good luck to the team!

Courtney

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/05/Screenshot-2025-05-02-at-12.20.52 PM.png]

#### 2025-05-04 — CRLS Crew – Weekly Update – 5/4/2025

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Volunteers · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls-crew-weekly-update-5-4-2025/>*

Hello, CRLS Crew families –

First off, great job to the team against Groton yesterday! Results are available here [https://www.row2k.com/results/resultspage.cfm?UID=FF45AC48CF863069B998EA93AF28A674&cat=5]. Thanks so much to the family volunteers who handled lunch prep & distribution, as well as to those who took care of set-up and break down of the tents!

We’ve got races every weekend until the end of May, so we are going to need everyone to step up to support the team. I’m communicating closely with all of the coaches to determine who will be racing when, as that dictates what we need to do to feed and support the kids. With the exception of the MPSRA state championship regatta on Sunday, May 25, it is likely that not every athlete will be rowing at every race. Depending on the number of kids rowing at a particular race, we will either make food or cater the event. So please stay tuned for additional details on how we will be handling each race. Again, we are going to need as much help as possible for the rest of the month, so please pitch in when you can!

Enjoy the rest of your Sunday! I will disseminate more info as soon as I can.

Best,

Courtney

#### 2025-05-17 — CRLS Crew – Weekly Update – 5/17/2025 – Regionals and Entropy Cup

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Race Schedule · <https://crlsrowing.org/8253-2/>*

Here is your update for the week.

REGIONALS UPDATE: ALL CANCELLED DUE TO WATER CONDITIONS 

REGIONALS – Saturday/Sunday, May 17-18, 2025 

The B1V and G1V/2V boats are competing this weekend at USRowing Northeast Youth Championships, a/k/a Regionals. [https://www.regattacentral.com/regatta/?job_id=9269&org_id=1001] The schedule [https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1m_O35JwB-x5mPgSeTvFlz0-dN_t8hYgZe9C0d8L2sis/edit?gid=0#gid=0] is still being finalized, but the kids will be in events 7 & 8. For those interested in following along, the livestream will be available here [https://www.overnght.com/sport/rowing]. Good luck, CRLS!

NOVICE RACE 

ENTROPY CUP – Saturday, May 17, 2025 

This Saturday, the lower boats will be competing against Brookline and Boston Latin at home in the Entropy Cup. Info from Coach Mary Kay is included below. I’ll provide the boys’ lineups when I get them; the girls’ lineups are as follows:

Girls Novice 8+

Cox- Vida 8-Nova, 7-Bea 6-Nina 5-Lila 4-Jordie 3-Iris 2-Acadia 1-Leah

Girls Novice 4+

Cox- Dahlia 4-Julia 3-Keira 2-Ahoo 1-Ellie

Spare-Annie (hotseat)

CRLS lower boats will be racing this Saturday morning on the Charles River against Brookline and Boston Latin School in the Powerhouse stretch. Start line is by Weeks Footbridge and the finish is on Magazine Beach, just beyond Riverside Boat Club. The best viewing will be from Western or River St Bridges or Magazine Beach. 

Athletes should arrive at CBC in their unisuits, dressed in appropriate layers for the weather with a water bottle by 8:45. Please make sure your athletes have eaten something for breakfast befor e they arrive. 

The first race starts from Weeks Footbridge at 10am. The last race should start about 11:15 -if everything goes according to the schedule; It may take a little longer if wind affects how the boats get aligned. 

Almost all boats will be racing more than once to allow the coxswains a chance to polish their steering skills. 

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/05/Screenshot-2025-05-17-at-9.32.55 AM.png] [https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/05/Screenshot-2025-05-17-at-9.33.08 AM.png]

#### 2025-05-21 — CRLS Crew – Update – 5/21/2025

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Race Schedule, Regatta, Regatta Food · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls-crew-update-5-21-2025/>*

Good afternoon, CRLS Crew families –

A few mid-week updates:

NEIRA [https://www.regattacentral.com/regatta/?job_id=9185] heat sheets are below. Racing on Saturday starts at 8:00am, with the last final at 5:18pm. It will be a long day!

The NEIRA parent handout is also attached. Of note: entry fees are $10 ($11 cashless). Parking [https://www.google.com/maps/place/10+N+Lake+Ave,+Worcester,+MA+01604/@42.2773292,-71.7604388,16z/data=!3m1!4b1!4m6!3m5!1s0x89e40880e8162a5b:0x17a77edfd90c342!8m2!3d42.2773292!4d-71.7578585!16s%2Fg%2F11whs_5l7x?entry=ttu&g_ep=EgoyMDI1MDUxMS4wIKXMDSoASAFQAw%3D%3D] is up the hill at the UMass Medical Center. You must also pay for parking!

As of today, Mass Publics [https://www.regattacentral.com/regatta/?job_id=9184&org_id=0] is still happening on Sunday in Lowell [https://www.google.com/maps/place/UMass+Lowell+Bellegarde+Boathouse/@42.6405384,-71.3524311,580m/data=!3m2!1e3!4b1!4m6!3m5!1s0x89e3a490ca414f71:0x8a186d015a72ccb6!8m2!3d42.6405384!4d-71.3524311!16s%2Fg%2F11df1v089f?entry=ttu&g_ep=EgoyMDI1MDUxNS4wIKXMDSoASAFQAw%3D%3D]. Everyone is watching the weather very carefully (rain impacts the flow of the river and brings debris), and I will update you as soon as possible if plans change.

The Mass Publics schedule is not out yet, but expect it to be very similar to last year, with sweep time trials and sculling finals in the morning, and sweep finals in the afternoon. Last year, racing started at 8 and ended around 4:30pm.

Volunteer sign-ups for the weekend are here [https://www.signupgenius.com/go/60B0F4CAAAA2CA31-56966031-neira#/]. Thank you to everyone who has already signed up and/or contributed food money. We also need to provide volunteers to the Mass Publics regatta organizers, so if you want to ride a launch or learn how a regatta is run, please let me know and I will forward the form. These jobs are also great for siblings!

Please RSVP here [https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLScjr-3VyHDSLjGylaLZJu_bQvxSY9L33SwqWBeyzelzNMxPVw/viewform?usp=dialog] to let us know if you are planning on coming to either race.

We are finalizing the exact time for next week’s end of the season BBQ. It will be sometime between 11:00am – 2:00pm on May 31 at the boathouse, but I’ll get the exact time out asap.

Finally, Arlington-Belmont needs our help! If you’re able to support a fellow public school team, go here [https://abcrewteam.org/fundraising/].

Thanks!

Courtney

Neira Heat Sheet:NEIRA 2025 Heat Sheet [https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/05/NEIRA-2025-Heat-Sheet.pdf]

Neira Parent Handout: NEIRA 2025 – Parent Handout [https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/05/NEIRA-2025-Parent-Handout.pdf]

#### 2025-05-22 — CRLS Crew – 5/22/2025 – Mass Publics Update (MPSRA)

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Regatta, Volunteers · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls-crew-5-22-2025-mass-publics-update-mpsra/>*

Good afternoon, CRLS Crew families –

Per my previous email, Mass Publics (Massachusetts Public School Rowing Association) will now be hosted by Watuppa in Fall River. [https://www.google.com/maps/place/Watuppa+Rowing+Center/@41.673721,-71.1425756,589m/data=!3m2!1e3!4b1!4m6!3m5!1s0x89e4ff0812e0c2eb:0xb0dab1fffc0b138e!8m2!3d41.673721!4d-71.1425756!16s%2Fg%2F11h3qrwlsq?entry=ttu&g_ep=EgoyMDI1MDUxNS4xIKXMDSoASAFQAw%3D%3D] Preliminary schedule is available here [https://legacy.herenow.com/results/#/races/21341/schedule].

I understand that Fall River is a longer drive, but this is preferable to canceling the event! We still need volunteers, so please sign uphere [https://www.signupgenius.com/go/60B0F4CAAAA2CA31-56966031-neira#/]. (If you need to change your volunteer role due to the change of venue, please do so by the close of business today!) Carroll and crew will reach out to volunteers to discuss logistics tonight or tomorrow. Stay tuned!

As noted before, regatta organizers are asking that each team provide a certain number of volunteers to help with regatta operations. We have to provide at least five volunteers. A sign-up form and job descriptions are available here [https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLSfH5LJkDxRqEi2bCht43wb68uC36W-UigbYJuNiXFEzsS9bRA/viewform]. South Watuppa Pond doesn’t offer the best views for spectators, so volunteering with race operations is a great way to see the action up close! And younger siblings make excellent stakeboat holders! If you opt to help out with race operations, please let me know so that I can ensure we are in compliance!

In summary, Mass Publics is still happening, and the team still needs our help! Thanks to everyone who has stepped up, and thanks so much for your patience as we pivot with respect to our new venue!

Questions? Feel free to reach out.

Thanks!

Courtney

#### 2025-05-23 — CRLS Crew – 5/23/2025 – NEIRA/MPSRA Update #3

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements · <https://crlsrowing.org/crls-crew-5-23-2025-neira-mpsra-update-3/>*

Hello CRLS Crew families –

Following are today’s NEIRA/MPSRA updates:

NEIRA – Saturday – Worcester – Varsity only 

The NEIRA buses will leave CBC at 6:00am. All athletes should be at the boathouse by 5:45am!

As a reminder, spectator entry fees are $10 cash/$11 cashless.

We will be providing breakfast/lunch/snacks for the kids. If you have volunteered to help with lunch, we will be grilling, so it’s helpful if you know how to grill!

Heat sheets are here [https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1jj-g2yTzucPVcGqm9sVFV03hgxQNIp_5QzBaeMMUmJU/edit?gid=1764859068#gid=1764859068]. Check with your athlete for lineups.

There is a paid livestream available at Overnght.com [https://www.overnght.com/].

MPSRA (Mass Publics) – Sunday – Fall River – Varsity & Novice 

The MPSRA buses will leave in 2 SHIFTS!

Bus 1 will leave CBC at 5:45am. Athletes on Bus 1 should be at CBC at 5:30am

Bus 2 will leave CBC at 9:00am . Athletes on Bus 2 should be at CBC at 8:45am.

The following boats must take Bus 1:

Girls 1V

Boys 1V

Girls 1st Novice 4

Boys 1st Novice 4

Boys Novice 2x

All other boats should take Bus 2.

We will be providing breakfast/lunch/snacks for the kids. If you have volunteered to help with lunch, we will be grilling, so it’s helpful if you know how to grill!

We are still looking for volunteers to help regatta organizers. Sign up here [https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLSfH5LJkDxRqEi2bCht43wb68uC36W-UigbYJuNiXFEzsS9bRA/viewform].

Schedule is here [https://legacy.herenow.com/results/#/races/21341/schedule]

Other helpful info: 

This will be a long weekend of rowing in less-than-ideal spring weather. Rowers should have extra warm/waterproof clothes and socks, and something to keep them occupied, like a book/homework/cards. Camping hammocks and light camp throws are great if you already own them!

All athletes should bring a filled water bottle!

Message from Coach Mary Kay: 

All novice girls are expected to race. Their line ups are listed below with their boats. 

There will be a first bus at 5:45 which will include the boys 2x, the 1V girls, 1V boys 1Novice boys and 1 Novice girls who all have to race time trials in the morning starting at 8:24 to determine which boats go to the Grand Final and which go to the Petite Final. The rest of the boats go straight to Final so they will be boarding a bus at 9am because their race is at 12:28. 

There will be food provided by parents at the race course; Breakfast and lunch for everyone. 

Things to remember: Wear your unisuit bring water bottle, hat, sunscreen, extra clothes to match the weather and to change out of wet clothes. 

You are expected to stay all day…to help assemble/de-rig your boat, assist with other crews equipment (like oars), to be on best behavior because you are representing the CRLS crew team, and to have fun.

#### 2025-06-08 — Registration for Fall 2025 is NOW OPEN

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Preseason, Season Registration · <https://crlsrowing.org/registration-for-fall-2025-is-now-open/>*

Registration for CRLS Crew for Fall 2025 [https://secure1.cpsd.us/athletics/] is now open via the Athletics Department. Please review the following dates and requirements for the fall season. The deadline to register for Fall Crew is August 13, 2025. 

Preseason: 

Returning Varsity: 

August 18-22, Timing TBD

August 25-29, Timing TBD

All new rowers: 

August 25-28, Timing TBD (normally mid-day practices)

All interested participants must complete and/or comply with the following items BEFORE the season begins. If ANY of these are not completed, the athlete will be sent home immediately and not allowed to return until everything is in order.

1) Maintain excellent Academic Standing [http://crls.cpsd.us/athletics/academic_eligibility] 

- Take a full course load of four classes

- Pass at least three of four 10-credit letter graded courses and earned a GPA of 70 or higher on the most recent report card

- You must receive passing grades in English, Physical Education, or Dance, if enrolled

2) Parent/Guardian registers you with CPSD Athletics [https://secure1.cpsd.us/athletics/] 

- Select either “Crew (Girls)” or “Crew (Boys)” regardless of Varsity or Novice

- Parental permission, agreement to participate, agreement to the handbook

- Understanding of the Hazing, Bullying, and Chemical Health rules

3) Submit the following documentation to the Athletics Office via email addressed to cpsathletics@cpsd.us. 

- Proof of Sports Physical Exam ( good for 13 months )

- Proof of Covid vaccination

- Concussion Course [https://nfhslearn.com/courses/61037] certificates from the athlete AND a parent/guardian ( good for 1 academic year )

- Implicit Bias Course [https://nfhslearn.com/courses/implicit-bias] certificate from the athlete (good for 1 academic year)

4) Pass a Swim Test ( good for 4 years ) 

- Administered by the team at the start of every season usually at War Memorial Pool.

- Swim 100 yards in a competent manner and remain afloat for 15 minutes unassisted

- Fulfills the swimming graduation requirement

5) Take the ImPACT Test ( good for 2 years ) 

- Computerized baseline concussion test given to athletes at the beginning of the season; details provided each season for anyone needing it.

- Please note: This test is emailed to the STUDENT EMAIL following registration with CPSD Athletics

Questions? 

Please email any inquiries to anyone on our coaching staff to: crls@cambridge-boat-club.org [mailto:crls@cambridge-boat-club.org]

#### 2025-07-23 — Registration for Fall 2025 closes August 13

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Preseason, Season Registration · <https://crlsrowing.org/registration-for-fall-2025-is-now-open-2/>*

Registration for CRLS Crew for Fall 2025 [https://secure1.cpsd.us/athletics/] is now open via the Athletics Department. Please review the following dates and requirements for the fall season. The deadline to register for Fall Crew is August 13, 2025.

- You must receive passing grades in English, Physical Education, or Dance, if enrolled

2) Parent/Guardian registers you with CPSD Athletics [https://secure1.cpsd.us/athletics/] 

- Select either “Crew (Girls)” or “Crew (Boys)” regardless of Varsity or Novice

- Parental permission, agreement to participate, agreement to the handbook

- Understanding of the Hazing, Bullying, and Chemical Health rules

3) Submit the following documentation to the Athletics Office via email addressed to cpsathletics@cpsd.us. 

- Proof of Sports Physical Exam ( good for 13 months )

- Proof of Covid vaccination

- Concussion Course [https://nfhslearn.com/courses/61037] certificates from the athlete AND a parent/guardian ( good for 1 academic year )

- Implicit Bias Course [https://nfhslearn.com/courses/implicit-bias] certificate from the athlete (good for 1 academic year)

4) Pass a Swim Test ( good for 4 years ) 

- Administered by the team at the start of every season usually at War Memorial Pool.

- Swim 100 yards in a competent manner and remain afloat for 15 minutes unassisted

- Fulfills the swimming graduation requirement

5) Take the ImPACT Test ( good for 2 years ) 

- Computerized baseline concussion test given to athletes at the beginning of the season; details provided each season for anyone needing it.

- Please note: This test is emailed to the STUDENT EMAIL following registration with CPSD Athletics

#### 2025-07-26 — Fall 2025 Preseason Schedule and more dates for your calendar

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Practice and Race Schedules, Preseason · <https://crlsrowing.org/fall-2025-preseason-schedule-and-more-dates-for-your-calendar/>*

It’s almost August and so we’ve got pre-season on the brain (although still enjoying summer!). There are some key dates that we want you to be aware of. Our website also has a full calendar [https://crlsrowing.org/what-you-need-to-know/calendar/] if you need to see the whole season line-up!

• CRLS Fall Sports Registration Deadline is Wednesday August 13, 2025 at 11 pm. This is a hard deadline with no extensions. ALL paperwork, including health forms and concussion and implicit bias course certificates of completion must be submitted by the deadline, or your athlete may not participate in tryouts or pre-season practices sessions.Please visit the CRLS Athletics site to register: https://secure1.cpsd.us/athletics/ [https://secure1.cpsd.us/athletics/]

• Returning Rowers (Varsity) Pre-Season Practice Schedule 

Monday August 18 – Friday, August 22. NO SATURDAY.

Monday August 25 – Thursday, August 28. FRI-MON OFF FOR LABOR DAY 

If you are a returning rower, and have at least one season of participation with CRLS, you are expected to attend the Varsity pre-season which will meet at regular practice times starting August 18th.

PRACTICE TIMES HERE (SAME AS SPRING 2025 SEASON) [https://crlsrowing.org/what-you-need-to-know/fall2025/]

• Novice Pre-Season Practice Schedule

Monday, August 25, 10 am – 12 pm : Orientation to team and equipment at CBC

Tuesday, August 26, 10 am – 12 pm : Barge at CBC

Wednesday, August 27, 3-5 pm – Swim test and Safety Video at CRLS* 

Thursday, August 28 – 1:30 – 3:30 pm. 8+s rowing at CBC

Novices are strongly urged to attend Pre-Season to learn about the team, the sport, and the procedures for practice but you will not be penalized if you are unable to attend . 

If you are a returning novice rower, and have at least one season of participation with CRLS, you are expected to attend the Varsity pre-season which will meet at regular practice times starting August 18th.

When school begins, Novices will practice 5 days a week after school: https://crlsrowing.org/what-you-need-to-know/fall2025/ [https://crlsrowing.org/what-you-need-to-know/fall2025/]

• Fall 2025 Regatta Schedule 

The 2025 Regatta Schedule is now live on our website [https://crlsrowing.org/what-you-need-to-know/calendar/]!

• Family Orientation – September 13, 2025 10:30-12:30 – Save the Date 

Please reserve the morning of Saturday, September 13 for an orientation session for new and returning crew families at Cambridge Boat Club. We are going to be mixing up how we engage our community this year, so we need everyone to attend who is able! Share some coffee and treats, meet fellow caregivers and sign up for your preferred subcommittee.

New families: Meet the coaches, tour the boathouse and learn more about the CRLS rowing program and what to expect for the upcoming season.

Got questions? We’ve got answers! Email crlsrowingteam@gmail.com [mailto:crlsrowingteam@gmail.com]

Izzy,

President, CRLS Crew Parent Board 2025-26

together with 

Carroll, Secretary and Co-Chair, Race Operations

Marcela, Treasurer

Amy, Co-Chair, Race Operations

Melissa, Merchandise and Gear

Victor and Shannon, Maintenance and Boathouse

Rodrigo, Race Operations Finances

Diane, Fundraising and Events

#### 2025-08-12 — One more day: Fall registration closes TOMORROW, Wednesday August 13

*Author: Izzy Ehrlich · Categories: Announcements, Boys Varsity, Girls Varsity, Season Registration · <https://crlsrowing.org/one-more-day-fall-registration-closes-tomorrow-wednesday-august-13/>*

The deadline for registering for Fall Crew is TOMORROW, Wednesday August 13 at 11 pm. This is a hard deadline with no extensions. 

ALL paperwork, including health forms and concussion and implicit bias course certificates of completion must be submitted by the deadline, or your athlete may not participate in tryouts or pre-season practices sessions. Please visit the CRLS Athletics site to register: https://secure1.cpsd.us/athletics/ [https://secure1.cpsd.us/athletics/]

Courses can be found here:

Implicit Bias: https://nfhslearn.com/courses/implicit-bias [https://nfhslearn.com/courses/implicit-bias]

Concussion: https://nfhslearn.com/courses/concussion-for-students [https://nfhslearn.com/courses/concussion-for-students]

We are so excited to see all of you and your athletes soon, so get on it and get that paperwork done!

Questions? Email us at crlsrowingteam@gmail.com [mailto:crlsrowingteam@gmail.com]; we are always here to help you out. (Except with the paperwork – you have to do that yourself.)
