### New Rowers – Fall 2025

*<https://crlsrowing.org/what-you-need-to-know/new-rowers-fall-2025/>*

The following information is for new rowers (novice*). All registered participants will be sent emails to their school accounts by the coaches in early August about the season and what to expect. 

*Novice rowers are new to the sport – they are often freshmen, but could be from any class at CRLS. 

Novice Pre-Season Practice Schedule

Monday, August 25, 10 am – 12 pm : Orientation to team and equipment at CBC

Tuesday, August 26, 10 am – 12 pm : Barge at CBC

Wednesday, August 27, 3-5 pm – Swim test and Safety Video at CRLS* 

Thursday, August 28 – 1:30 – 3:30 pm. 8+s rowing at CBC

Novices are strongly urged to attend Pre-Season to learn about the team, the sport, and the procedures for practice but you will not be penalized if you are unable to attend . If you are a returning rower, and have at least one season of participation with CRLS, you are expected to attend the Varsity pre-season which will meet at regular practice times starting August 18th.

When school begins, Novices will practice 5 days a week after school: https://crlsrowing.org/what-you-need-to-know/fall2025/

All interested participants must complete and/or comply with the following items BEFORE the season begins. If ANY of these are not completed, the athlete will be sent home immediately and not allowed to return until everything is in order.

1) Maintain excellent Academic Standing [http://crls.cpsd.us/athletics/academic_eligibility] 

- Take a full course load of four classes

- Pass at least three of four 10-credit letter graded courses and earned a GPA of 70 or higher on the most recent report card

- You must receive passing grades in English, Physical Education, or Dance, if enrolled

2) Parent/Guardian registers you with CPSD Athletics [https://secure1.cpsd.us/athletics/] 

- Select either “Crew (Girls)” or “Crew (Boys)” regardless of Varsity or Novice

- Parental permission, agreement to participate, agreement to the handbook

- Understanding of the Hazing, Bullying, and Chemical Health rules

3) Submit the following documentation to the Athletics Office via email addressed to cpsathletics@cpsd.us. 

- Proof of Sports Physical Exam ( good for 13 months )

- Proof of Covid vaccination

- Concussion Course [https://nfhslearn.com/courses/61037] certificates from the athlete ( good for 1 academic year )

- Implicit Bias Course [https://nfhslearn.com/courses/implicit-bias] certificate from the athlete (good for 1 academic year)

4) Pass a Swim Test ( good for 4 years ) 

- Administered by the team at the start of every season usually at War Memorial Pool.

- Swim 100 yards in a competent manner and remain afloat for 15 minutes unassisted

- Fulfills the swimming graduation requirement

5) Take the ImPACT Test ( good for 2 years ) 

- Computerized baseline concussion test given to athletes at the beginning of the season; details provided each season for anyone needing it.

- Please note: This test is emailed to the STUDENT EMAIL following registration with CPSD Athletics

6) Complete waiver for Cambridge Boat Club (CBC) 

- Our home boathouse is CBC and they require this waiver [https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/CRLS-Waiver-and-Release-rev-07-21-22.pdf] be signed by all participating athletes. Please email this to the coaches at crls@cambridge-boat-club.org [mailto:crls@cambridge-boat-club.org] or turn it during the first week of practice.

Questions? 

Please email any inquiries to anyone on our coaching staff to: crls@cambridge-boat-club.org [mailto:crls@cambridge-boat-club.org]

### New Rowers – Fall 2026

*<https://crlsrowing.org/what-you-need-to-know/new-rowers-fall-2026/>*

The following information is for new rowers (novice*). All registered participants will be sent emails to their school accounts by the coaches in early August about the season and what to expect. 

*Novice rowers are new to the sport – they are often freshmen, but could be from any class at CRLS. 

Novices will practice 5 days a week after school: https://crlsrowing.org/what-you-need-to-know/fall2026/ [https://crlsrowing.org/what-you-need-to-know/fall2026/]

Registration for CRLS Crew for Fall 2026 [https://secure1.cpsd.us/athletics/] is now open via the Athletics Department. Please review the following dates and requirements for the fall season. The deadline to register for Fall Crew is August 17, 2026. 

Please note that the Agust 17 is a HARD DEADLINE and ALL steps must be completed before PreSeason which starts August 24th. You cannot register your athlete past this date, so please make this a priority!

All interested participants must complete and/or comply with the following items BEFORE the season begins. If ANY of these are not completed, the athlete will be sent home immediately and not allowed to return until everything is in order.

1) Maintain excellent Academic Standing [http://crls.cpsd.us/athletics/academic_eligibility] 

- Take a full course load of four classes

- Pass at least three of four 10-credit letter graded courses and earned a GPA of 70 or higher on the most recent report card

- You must receive passing grades in English, Physical Education, or Dance, if enrolled

2) Parent/Guardian registers you with CPSD Athletics [https://secure1.cpsd.us/athletics/] 

- Select either “Crew (Girls)” or “Crew (Boys)” regardless of Varsity or Novice

- Parental permission, agreement to participate, agreement to the handbook

- Understanding of the Hazing, Bullying, and Chemical Health rules

3) Submit the following documentation to the Athletics Office via this registration link [https://portal.cpsd.us/athletics/home.php]. 

- Proof of Sports Physical Exam ( good for 13 months )

- Concussion Education Course [https://www.nfhslearn.com/] certificates from the athlete AND a parent/guardian ( good for 1 academic year )

- Implicit Bias Course [https://www.nfhslearn.com/] certificate from the athlete (good for 1 academic year)

- Impact Testing, After registration, you will receive an email from a member of the CRLS Athletics Training Staff, that email includes directions and instructions for accessing the ImPact Test. It is important to read it carefully and execute the test appropriately. Once completed, the Athletic Training Staff will record this in your profile. ImPact Test baseline scores are valid for 2 years.

4) Pass a Swim Test ( good for 4 years ) 

- Administered by the team at the start of every season usually at War Memorial Pool.

- Swim 100 yards in a competent manner and remain afloat for 15 minutes unassisted

- Fulfills the swimming graduation requirement

5) Take the ImPACT Test ( good for 2 years ) 

- After registration, you will receive an email from a member of the CRLS Athletics Training Staff, that email includes directions and instructions for accessing the ImPact Test. It is important to read it carefully and execute the test appropriately. Once completed, the Athletic Training Staff will record this in your profile.

- Please note: This test is emailed to the STUDENT EMAIL following registration with CPSD Athletics.

5) Note from coaches 

- Depending upon numbers, cuts may have to be made; will be based on getting all registration paperwork completed on time, fitness levels, attitude, coachability, commitment to attending practice and races. Cuts will be announced around September 11th.

6) Complete waiver for Cambridge Boat Club (CBC) 

- Our home boathouse is CBC and they require this waiver [https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/CRLS-Waiver-and-Release-rev-07-21-22.pdf] be signed by all participating athletes. Please email this to the coaches at crls@cambridge-boat-club.org [mailto:crls@cambridge-boat-club.org] or turn it during the first week of practice.

### Practice Locations and Transportation

*<https://crlsrowing.org/what-you-need-to-know/practice-locations-and-transportation/>*

Practice Locations: 

Outdoor Practice: 

Our program practices out of Cambridge Boat Club [https://www.cambridge-boat-club.org/] on the Charles River.

Indoor Practice: 

Indoor practices are held at Russell Field:

Swim Tests: 

Swim tests are held at War Memorial Recreation Center:

Transportation: 

The fastest way to get to practice is by bike or walking. Buses/trains can run late.

Please note: The school DOES NOT provide transport to practices, only to competitions.

### Results

*<https://crlsrowing.org/what-you-need-to-know/results/>*

Results

Nearly all regatta organizers use either RegattaCentral [https://www.regattacentral.com/] or HereNow [https://www.herenow.com/#/home] for their race management needs. These sites have all the info you need to know about a given regatta, including the race schedule, entries, heat sheets, parking and spectator information, and the results. Results [https://www.herenow.com/results/#/races/21042/results] are usually updated in real time, so you will know as your rower crosses the finish line how they did. Familiarize yourself with both sites now, and impress your rower with your knowledge of their event!

### Uniforms & Clothing

*<https://crlsrowing.org/what-you-need-to-know/uniforms/>*

General Information 

Practices can be cold so expect to wear layers and limit the amount of cotton. Windbreakers are very helpful in the beginning of this season. Close fitting clothes are best so they don’t get caught in the equipment. We also recommend a hat/visor and sunglasses, as well as sunscreen.

Practice: The students should acquire: rowing shorts/running shorts for warmer practices and tights for cold practices. Socks must be worn in the boat. Please bring athletic shoes for running to every practice.

Athletes get wet from splashing oars and wet boats, so clothing should NOT be cotton. Cotton stays wet longer than synthetic material and therefore is colder. Practice is mostly outdoors so athletes need to be warm even when wet.

Racing: The school will provide a rowing unisuit to be worn for races which must be returned at the end of the season.

Miscellaneous clothing notes: 

– Socks should be wool or synthetic in the cold seasons. Rowers strap into shoes that are shared in the boat so socks are always necessary.

– Warm hats are important in cold weather and baseball caps and sun glasses help keep the sun off in warm weather.

– Running shoes should be worn in the event of any dryland workouts.

Shopping 

Winter running and biking clothing is also often appropriate for cold weather rowing. Some local places to purchase warm lycra or spandex clothing locally are:

- Dicks Sporting Goods

- New Balance Outlet

- Local Bike shops

- Target [http://www.target.com/] in Cambridge, Somerville or Watertown

- REI [http://rei.com] in Boston or Reading

- At the Head of the Charles finish line expedition tent, rowing vendors sell very reasonably priced rowing clothing.

Coach Favorites for cold weather 

- vests

- splash jackets

- long tops

- tights

- pogies/socks/hats

Online Stores 

- JL Rowing [http://shop.jlathletics.com/index.php]

- Sew Sporty [http://www.sewsporty.com/crlscrew/index.php?main_page=index&cPath=13]

- Aero Tech Designs [http://www.aerotechdesigns.com/] (bike clothing)

### USRowing SafeSport Program

*<https://crlsrowing.org/what-you-need-to-know/usrowingsafesport/>*

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/download.jpg]

In October 2013, USRowing adopted new internal policies surrounding SafeSport and joined the United States Olympic Committee in its SafeSport campaign aimed at raising awareness, stopping child abuse in sport and creating a safe culture in sports programs across the country. As an organizational member of USRowing, CRLS Crew is proud to be a part of USRowing and their SafeSport campaign due to our own systems already in place.

– All coaches and student-athletes must agree to and submit registration [https://secure1.cpsd.us/athletics/] via the school portal which includes items that detail :

- Student Eligibility: time allowed for participation after first entering grade nine

- MIAA Bona Fide Team Member Waiver Guidelines

- MIAA Rule 62 Student Eligibility: Chemical Health/Alcohol/Drugs/Tobacco

- Student-Athlete Code of Conduct

- Media Release

- Consent for Medical Treatment

- State Law regarding Sports Related Head Injury & Concussions

- Commonwealth of Massachusetts General Laws 269:17, 18, 19 Hazing; Organizing or Participating; Hazing Defined

- Informed Consent and Release for Participant Agreement

– All crew coaching staff must pass a Massachusetts Criminal Offender Record Information (CORI) background check. All coaching staff have completed SafeSport training, CPR/AED training and other training.

### Crew Winter Training Program

*<https://crlsrowing.org/wintertraining/>*

Athletes are encouraged to participate in another sport during the winter but may instead join the crew conditioning program. Staying fit is a year round activity!

CRLS crew coaches run a winter conditioning program 5 days/week from Dec through the beginning of March using ergs, some weight training, cardio and FUN! A fee is charged but scholarships are available. Please ask the coaches for more information. 

[https://crlsrowing.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/IMG_2962-scaled.jpeg]

---

## 10. Explicit UNKNOWNs

- **Individual boat/shell names** — the site names no shells at all. No christening posts, no boat-naming, no fleet list.
- **Team colours** — never stated. The site says the team rowed in 'its own name and colors' from Spring 2000 but never says what those colours are.
- **Team motto** — none published.
- **The 'major anniversary' referenced in an October 2023 board post** — never identified.
- **Full names for some coaches** (Bailey, Aleks in 2025; Matt, Anna, Dan, James, Emily in 2012-13) and for many novice athletes listed by first name only.
- **Bios for most current coaches** — the coaching page is a name/role/email list only; the biographical detail for Becklean, Bauer, Smith and Fellows comes from news posts, not bios.
- **Bill Becklean Award recipients** — the award exists but no winners are listed.
- **Team Awards after 2015-2016** — the awards tables stop there.
- **Trophy Case after 2016-2017** — season result pages end at Spring 2017.
- **Cambridge Latin School founding year** — the site gives both 1643 (live History page) and 1641 (archived Championships page).
- **Alumni destinations map** — the 'Where in the world is CRLS Crew?' page is an embedded iframe with no extractable text.
- **The Hammer erg-record page** — an embedded Google Sheet; contents not retrievable as text.
- **Password-protected content** — the Team Login area, Photos pages and Committees page are password protected and were not accessed.
